Entry points: `professional_words_ui.spec.ts` reads fifteen pages as rendered in Chromium, server words included, for a retired word, and finds the renamed controls at 1440 and 400 px; `professional_words_api.spec.ts` checks the refusals and the stop-reason table over HTTP. **Partial (DB-N23-5):** the triage, Accept, reopen and revert refusals name the board's columns (`triage.ts`, `accept.ts` through `boardColumnLabel`; `professional_words_api.spec.ts`), but the kernel's and the board's own refusals (`Illegal transition '…' -> '…'`, `is in '…', not '…'`, `card_store.ts`, `board_service.ts`), which reach the page only through an override, still quote stored states, and no test reads every refusal the server can return. **Kept by this spec, not renamed:** *Project manager* in the nav (§2.2.1; R-11 proposed *AI project manager*), `g w` and the one-release chord note (§2.3.1; R-40), *Pipeline stages* (item 5). **Left to C5:** the dev help's *bake-off*, *research-bakeoff* and *golden set* (R-21), the README and INSTALL.md (R-33). **Model-facing, not renamed (PROMPT_STANDARD rule 35):** the MCP tools' descriptions (`mcp.ts`, WRD-01), Seshat's tool descriptions and the Agent's dossier lines | NEW-dashboard-23 |

## 5. Changes for v1

### P3 — A professional board
*The columns are the machine's nine states, the tile shows harness telemetry first, and nothing starts without the CLI.*

- **DB-P3-1** WHEN the board renders THE SYSTEM SHALL show Backlog, To do, In progress, In review and Done, plus On hold only when a card is parked, and map every stored state to exactly one of them as in §2.4.1.
- **DB-P3-2** WHEN a card in verify has a failing gate THE SYSTEM SHALL show it in In progress with a fail-tone badge naming the gate.
- **DB-P3-3** WHEN pipeline stages are toggled on THE SYSTEM SHALL show the nine stored states as columns and keep that choice after a reload.
- **DB-P3-4** WHEN a tile renders THE SYSTEM SHALL show the issue key, type icon, points (when set), owner, delegate chip (when a delegate is set), title, priority glyph (only when priority ≠ 0), epic chip, up to two labels, the status badge and, for In progress and In review, the work item age.
- **DB-P3-5** WHEN a tile's delegate is the Worker THE SYSTEM SHALL show the text chip *Worker* and no avatar or letter badge.
- **DB-P3-6** WHEN a card has a `blockedReason` or an unfinished dependency THE SYSTEM SHALL show a blocker flag in the fail tone with the link icon, the word *Blocked* and its cause.
- **DB-P3-7** WHEN the status text is longer than one line THE SYSTEM SHALL wrap it and never cut off the cause.
- **DB-P3-8** WHEN a card is in Backlog or Ready THE SYSTEM SHALL show no step budget on the board face.
- **DB-P3-9** WHEN the In review limit is computed THE SYSTEM SHALL show `count / limit` and its derivation whatever the limit's size, and every column header SHALL show its points sum.
- **DB-P3-10** WHEN a column is empty THE SYSTEM SHALL show it as a chip above the board, and Done SHALL be a full column whenever it has cards, at every width.
- **DB-P3-11** WHEN In review or On hold renders THE SYSTEM SHALL order its cards by wait time, longest first.
- **DB-P3-12** WHEN a person presses `c` or a column's `+` THE SYSTEM SHALL open a create form whose result is a proposal from the planner pipeline, and no message SHALL tell them to use the CLI.
- **DB-P3-13** WHEN a board has epics with slices THE SYSTEM SHALL render `#/board/map` with epics in backbone order and the first slice marked.
- **DB-P3-14** WHEN a cycle is in force THE SYSTEM SHALL render a burn-up with separate done and scope lines; WHEN the filter is `cycle:none` THE SYSTEM SHALL hide the cycle header.
- **DB-P3-15** WHEN a focused card changes column on a stream frame THE SYSTEM SHALL keep focus on it and scroll it into view, and SHALL scroll no other card.
- **DB-P3-16** WHEN the page is reloaded mid-run THE SYSTEM SHALL show the same columns, tiles and statuses as a page that stayed open.
- **DB-P3-17** WHEN a developer uses the board at 1440 and 1100 px THE SYSTEM SHALL let them find which card is blocked and why within three actions (DEFINITION_OF_DONE §6.4).
- **DB-P3-18** WHEN On hold holds cards THE SYSTEM SHALL show it as the right-most column with its count in `--state-parked`; WHEN it holds none THE SYSTEM SHALL show no On hold column.

### P4 — The Learn layer
*The claims table says "not built"; teaching exists only in hover tooltips.*

- **DB-P4-1** WHEN Learn is off THE SYSTEM SHALL render zero Learn nodes and the same layout as without the feature.
- **DB-P4-2** WHEN Learn is on THE SYSTEM SHALL show a *?* on every column header, WIP count, gate pip group, points field, cycle header, Insights chart and story-map slice.
- **DB-P4-3** WHEN a *?* is activated by keyboard THE SYSTEM SHALL open a popover with the concept, a line computed from the project's own numbers, and a link to its canonical source, and `Esc` SHALL return focus to the *?*.
- **DB-P4-4** WHEN the In review limit is 3 at 60 review minutes a day THE SYSTEM SHALL say so in that popover with those numbers.
- **DB-P4-5** WHEN `learn.ts` is tested THE SYSTEM SHALL have a lesson for every board column, every gate family and every Insights metric.
- **DB-P4-6** WHEN Learn is on and a Contract card's kind is explained THE SYSTEM SHALL call it an enabler and SHALL NOT name a SPIDR slice for it.
- **DB-P4-7** WHEN a person answers the first-run question with "I'm learning" THE SYSTEM SHALL turn Learn on; with "I write code" it SHALL stay off.
- **DB-P4-8** WHEN a beginner with Learn on starts from the board THE SYSTEM SHALL let them reach the explanation of a WIP limit by keyboard alone (DEFINITION_OF_DONE §6.4).

*Status (B4.7):* on screen the layer is *Tips* (DEC-31). DB-P4-1 and DB-P4-3..7 are built and tested in `packages/ui/tests/learn.spec.ts` (the lessons, their lines, the button's markup, the first run); DB-P4-2 is partial — no `?` on the points field (the issue page's and the peek's *Points*) or the list view's Points column, only on the column's points sum and the sprint header —; DB-P4-1, -3, -7 and -8 in the page are `apps/harness/tests/tips_ui.spec.ts` (Chromium), written but not yet run. DB-P4-2's gate pip group is taught from the Checks strip, not the tile (§4, the Learn layer row).

### P5 — Status for non-developers, and starting a project without a terminal
*Non-developers land in jargon; the standup, signals and goals APIs are unused; every "start" path ends at a terminal.*

- **DB-P5-1** WHEN a person opens `#/status` THE SYSTEM SHALL show a plain-language headline, the proven count per slice, a burn-up, *Needs you* with buttons, today's standup in plain mode, the fired signals as sentences, an Ask box and a **Start a new project** button (the rest of the page is DB-N9-1).
- **DB-P5-2** WHEN any Status or plain-mode text is rendered THE SYSTEM SHALL contain no stop-reason code, card id without a title, or gate id without words.
- **DB-P5-3** WHEN **Start a new project** is pressed THE SYSTEM SHALL open Seshat with the start-project conversation, and applying its proposal group SHALL create the project's cards with no terminal step.
- **DB-P5-4** WHEN the palette query is "new project" THE SYSTEM SHALL offer *Start a new project*; WHEN a query matches nothing THE SYSTEM SHALL offer *Ask Seshat: <query>*.
- **DB-P5-5** WHEN the composer's cost line renders THE SYSTEM SHALL contain no API path and no model name or id.
- **DB-P5-6** WHEN the Seshat panel header renders THE SYSTEM SHALL show *Seshat · Project manager* and the presence line, and SHALL show no model name or id in the header or its details.
- **DB-P5-7** WHEN a non-developer at 400 px wide starts a project and then asks how it is going THE SYSTEM SHALL complete both without a terminal (DEFINITION_OF_DONE §6.4).

### P11 — The navigation
*14 flat items, labels lost at laptop widths, no phone navigation, chords that are not mnemonics, a bare `t`.*

- **DB-P11-1** WHEN the dashboard has no runs, no dependency edges and one project THE SYSTEM SHALL show no Runs or Dependencies item, and SHALL show Projects, Inbox and Configuration (superseded in part by DEC-35: Projects, which replaces Workspace, is always shown).
- **DB-P11-2** WHEN the window is 1100 px wide THE SYSTEM SHALL show every nav label.
- **DB-P11-3** WHEN the window is 400 px wide THE SYSTEM SHALL show a bottom bar with Status, Review, Board and PM, each reachable in one tap.
- **DB-P11-4** WHEN a person presses `g` then a letter THE SYSTEM SHALL go to the visible view the keymap assigns that chord; every visible view SHALL have exactly one chord, no two views SHALL share one, and `t` alone SHALL do nothing.
- **DB-P11-5** WHEN Playbook, Runs or Integrations is visible THE SYSTEM SHALL reach it with `g k`, `g u` or `g n` respectively, and from the palette.
- **DB-P11-6** WHEN the cheat sheet opens in a 900 px-high window THE SYSTEM SHALL show every key group without clipping, and every entry SHALL come from the same keymap as the palette.
- **DB-P11-7** WHEN decisions are waiting THE SYSTEM SHALL list them under Review › *Needs you* and in the Inbox's *Needs you* group, and each Inbox item SHALL open the decision in Review (superseded in part by DEC-35: `#/inbox` is the Inbox page, no longer an alias of that group).

*Status (B4.2):* DB-P11-1, -2, -4, -5 and -6 are built and tested (`nav.spec.ts`, `web_css.spec.ts`), and were checked in a browser at 1100×800, 1440×900 and 400×812. DB-P11-3 is built: the bar reads Status · Review · Board · PM (`BOTTOM_BAR`, `nav.ts:162`; B4.7). The first-run role question is built (B4.7); its state, and DB-P11-7's, are §4's navigation and first-run rows (C1 re-mark: this paragraph was B4.2's).

### P12 — Colour and contrast
*Gold and warning amber share a hue; disabled Accept is 2.1:1; input borders 1.3–1.9:1; placeholders carry instructions in muted text.*

- **DB-P12-1** WHEN the token test runs THE SYSTEM SHALL assert every text pair as used ≥ 4.5:1, body text (`--text-primary` on base and surface) ≥ 7:1, and every control edge, state icon and the accent ≥ 3:1, in both themes.
- **DB-P12-2** WHEN the parked/warning hue and the accent hue are compared THE SYSTEM SHALL find at least 20° of hue between them in both themes.
- **DB-P12-3** WHEN a button is disabled THE SYSTEM SHALL render it with a neutral fill, and its reason as adjacent text.
- **DB-P12-4** WHEN any input renders THE SYSTEM SHALL edge it with `--border-control`.
- **DB-P12-5** WHEN any placeholder or information-bearing text renders THE SYSTEM SHALL NOT use `--text-muted`.
- **DB-P12-6** WHEN the accessibility check runs over every route at 400, 1100 and 1440 px in both themes THE SYSTEM SHALL find no control without an accessible name, no target under 24×24 px on desktop or under 44×44 px at 400 px, no layout that breaks at 200% zoom, and no information available only on hover.
- **DB-P12-7** WHEN a tile has keyboard focus THE SYSTEM SHALL draw the 2 px accent outline at the global 1 px offset, unclipped by its column, as on every other control.

*Status (B4.2):* DB-P12-1, -2, -4, -5 and -7 are built and tested (`tokens.spec.ts`, `web_css.spec.ts`), with DEC-42's parked/fail rule (§2.13.2) in `tokens.spec.ts`. DB-P12-3 is **partial**: built for the neutral fill and for triage, where the offline reason sits beside the buttons, and seen for GitHub sync (the not-connected line above its buttons) and stale proposals (*Out of date*); the other disabled buttons (mostly busy states with a label such as *Sending…*) have not been audited one by one, and no test covers the adjacent-reason half. DB-P12-6 is **built and tested** (`apps/harness/tests/a11y.spec.ts`, run by the gate's vitest: playwright-core 1.61.1 drives the cached Chromium build 1228, axe-core 4.13.0 pinned; about 35 s) — with one refinement of the hover rule, argued in the file: a control inside a closed disclosure is judged by the text it shows once open, since it cannot be hovered while closed. It proves the routes as seeded with two cards; the views it cannot reach with that data are listed in §4.

### P13 — Where "done" is drawn (page side)
*The requirement graph, release slices, test strength and appetite that decide a project's done are drawn nowhere ([PROJECT_DONE_AND_DEPTH](../../research/PROJECT_DONE_AND_DEPTH.md); behaviour in [planner-pm §2.15](planner-pm.md)).*

- **DB-P13-1** WHEN a project has a requirement graph THE SYSTEM SHALL show its story map with the backbone of user activities, the release slices as horizontal bands, and each requirement as proven, passing with strength unmet, planned, unplanned, suspect or cut.
- **DB-P13-2** WHEN the Status view shows a project THE SYSTEM SHALL state the proven count of must-have requirements per release in DEC-31's words ("Release 1 · 9 of 11 requirements done"), count requirements whose tests pass without meeting their strength rule apart from the proven ones, and state the appetite used, in plain words.
- **DB-P13-3** WHEN a requirement's tests pass but its test-strength record does not meet the profile's rule THE SYSTEM SHALL show *Tests too weak* (DEC-52; *Passing, strength unmet* before it) on the story map and in Status, and SHALL NOT show it as proven (research TESTS_BROWNFIELD PM-TQ-8).
- **DB-P13-4** WHEN a requirement has been revised since a card or test was linked to it THE SYSTEM SHALL mark it *Needs re-checking* (DEC-52; *Suspect* before it) on the story map and in the card's Plan tab.
- **DB-P13-5** WHEN a slice reaches its appetite THE SYSTEM SHALL show Seshat's three choices (accept as proven, cut named nice-to-haves, extend) as buttons in Needs you.
- **DB-P13-6** WHEN every card of a project is Done but a chosen slice has not been accepted by a person THE SYSTEM SHALL NOT describe the project as done anywhere, and Status SHALL show the slice awaiting acceptance in Needs you.
- **DB-P13-7** WHEN a release is proposed for a slice THE SYSTEM SHALL show its notes and changelog in Needs you with a *Tag the release* button, and SHALL tag nothing without that press.
- **DB-P13-8** WHEN tests await a person's approval under the depth profile THE SYSTEM SHALL list them in Needs you and render each as its example table (given → expected) with Approve.
- **DB-P13-9** WHEN the Learn layer is on THE SYSTEM SHALL explain the walking skeleton, release slices, must-have versus nice-to-have, appetite and *strength unmet* where they appear.

### T5 — The dashboard server split by route group (page side)
*The UI is served by one ~1,045-line closure; the server split itself is [runtime](runtime.md)'s.*

- **DB-T5-1** WHEN the route table is enumerated in a test THE SYSTEM SHALL resolve every endpoint in §3's consumed list to exactly one handler module, and the existing server specs SHALL pass unchanged.

### S3c — Dashboard hardening (page side)
- **DB-S3c-1** WHEN a mutating request is sent without this page's session token THE SYSTEM SHALL refuse it, and the page SHALL send the token on every mutation it makes.
- **DB-S3c-2** WHEN the page is loaded THE SYSTEM SHALL run with a Content-Security-Policy that allows no inline script and `frame-ancestors 'none'`, and every view SHALL still work.
- **DB-S3c-3** WHEN model or repository text contains HTML THE SYSTEM SHALL render it as text in every view.

### NEW-dashboard-1 — Evidence that stays readable
*The gates strip, the product's central evidence element, breaks at 14 gates and on a phone (domain17 §2, top change 4). Not in COVERAGE.*

- **DB-N1-1** WHEN a card has 14 gates in 5 families THE SYSTEM SHALL show 5 grouped segments, failing groups first, and a *+n passed* overflow.
- **DB-N1-2** WHEN the strip renders at 400 px THE SYSTEM SHALL render it as a vertical list with no overlapping text.
- **DB-N1-3** WHEN a card in Planning has failing evidence THE SYSTEM SHALL show one badge describing its current state (*Needs a new plan*), never a failure mark on a stage name.
- **DB-N1-4** WHEN a card's evidence ran no Parse gate and `gates.toml` declares none THE SYSTEM SHALL show no Parse segment or pip for it; a declared Parse that did not run SHALL read *Not run*, never *Passed* (§2.5.3).

### NEW-dashboard-2 — A web layer under test, with one vocabulary
*About 60 modules are checked for syntax only; four status maps; wave-named CSS; a hard-coded integrations roadmap (domain13 §3, §5). Not in COVERAGE beyond T9's general gaps.*

- **DB-N2-1** WHEN the board, tile and nav models are tested THE SYSTEM SHALL assert exact outputs for every stored state, including at least two negative cases per model.
- **DB-N2-2** WHEN the ledger fails verification THE SYSTEM SHALL disable Accept on Review, the card view and the peek drawer, asserted by a test.
- **DB-N2-3** WHEN the repository is searched for status-to-label maps THE SYSTEM SHALL find exactly one, in `vocabulary.ts`, and its label for `in_progress` SHALL be *In progress*.
- **DB-N2-4** WHEN a card's kind is shown THE SYSTEM SHALL show its issue type (`issueTypeOf`, [DEC-31](../DECISIONS.md#dec-31); the kind labels of [NAMING](../NAMING.md#card-kind-change-and-split) are internal), and `vocabulary.ts` SHALL declare no kind enumeration of its own: an older card's title suffix is read as the kernel's stored kinds, *Visual* and *Integration* as `implement`. *(Amended by C5, 2026-09-27: the criterion named the kind labels DEC-31 retired from every screen.)*
- **DB-N2-5** WHEN a card has a `change` or a `split` THE SYSTEM SHALL label it from the same map, and SHALL NOT infer a card's `split` from its `kind` or its title.
- **DB-N2-6** WHEN the vocabulary is asked for a stop reason it does not know THE SYSTEM SHALL return its humanised enum.
- **DB-N2-7** WHEN `/api/integrations` omits an integration THE SYSTEM SHALL NOT show it; WHEN it returns entries with tiers THE SYSTEM SHALL group them as Now, Next and Later, and Next and Later cards SHALL have no button.
- **DB-N2-8** WHEN each state named in §2.7.8 (pending rows), §2.10.3–4 (capability and stopping policy) and §2.11 (Playbook, profile, Machine roster) is rendered from a fixture THE SYSTEM SHALL produce its specified text, asserted by a test, including the 404 state of each endpoint.
- **DB-N2-9** WHEN Machine renders THE SYSTEM SHALL show the active hardware tier.
- **DB-N2-10** WHEN a card in verify is running a check THE SYSTEM SHALL name that check in its In progress badge (*Running Tests…*); WHEN no check has started THE SYSTEM SHALL say *Running checks…* (DEC-31's word for a gate).
- **DB-N2-11** WHEN a run is in progress THE SYSTEM SHALL show its row on Runs as *Running · <finished> of <total> issues · <elapsed>* (DEC-31's word), updated from the stream without a reload.

### NEW-dashboard-3 — The model's output, live, on the Steps tab
*The server streams `event: tokens` but no page listens, so a long model call looks like a stall (inventory M2; `server.ts:1164`, `app.js:188-246`).*

- **DB-N3-1** WHEN the Steps tab of a running card is open and the stream sends `tokens` frames for that card THE SYSTEM SHALL append their text to the current step row, with no animation, and SHALL replace it with the step's summary when its `card/step` event arrives.
- **DB-N3-2** WHEN the Steps tab is not open THE SYSTEM SHALL keep no token text in memory.

### NEW-dashboard-4 — Settings, as sections of Configuration
*The Settings view (theme, density, review minutes per day, read-only configuration) was dropped without a Later entry (trace pm_fe row 347; DEC-25 R18). It is kept as three sections of the Configuration page (§2.16; owner decision O2).*

- **DB-N4-1** WHEN a person opens `#/settings`, or Configuration's This browser, Review capacity or Project configuration section, THE SYSTEM SHALL show theme, density, Learn and first-run role, and the project's review minutes per day and effective configuration with each value's source.
- **DB-N4-2** WHEN a person holding the Accept permission, or an Admin in the Team setup, changes review minutes per day THE SYSTEM SHALL record the change as an event with the principal and show the recomputed ReviewWIP at once; WHEN a person without it tries THE SYSTEM SHALL disable the field with that reason beside it.
- **DB-N4-3** WHEN a person enters 0 or less as review minutes per day THE SYSTEM SHALL refuse the value beside the field, keep the previous value, and leave ReviewWIP unchanged ([review-git](review-git.md) RG-S6-8).

*Status (C5, 2026-09-27):* DB-N4-1..3 are built and tested (`settings.spec.ts`; `review_capacity.spec.ts` over a real ledger, card store and board). NEW-dashboard-1: DB-N1-1..4 built (`gate_strip.spec.ts`, `web_css.spec.ts`). NEW-dashboard-2: DB-N2-1..7, -9 and -11 built (`web_models.spec.ts`, `accept_state.spec.ts`, `one_vocabulary.spec.ts`, `vocabulary.spec.ts`, `integrations_view.spec.ts`, `machine_tier.spec.ts`, `runs_running.spec.ts`; DB-N2-6 was already built, `vocabulary.spec.ts` *falls back to the humanised enum*); DB-N2-8 partial — its pending rows and Playbook and profile states built in B4.8 (`pending_rows.spec.ts`, `playbook_states.spec.ts`), the legacy Playbook view's words and the Machine and Insights 404 states still in their page modules — and DB-N2-10 built in B4.11 (`gate_announce.spec.ts`, `live_gate.spec.ts`, `running_check.spec.ts`), as §4 says.

### NEW-dashboard-5 — Review for a team, and review that forces a look
*Files are grouped alphabetically, the Reviewer's coverage is invisible, Accept can be pressed without looking, and a team cannot filter by who owns or builds a card ([DESIGN_RESEARCH_TEAMS_DATA_CHANGE](../../research/DESIGN_RESEARCH_TEAMS_DATA_CHANGE.md) decisions 1, 16; DB-T1…T6; TESTS_BROWNFIELD decisions 6, 8).*

- **DB-N5-1** WHEN a card's diff is shown in Review THE SYSTEM SHALL order Implementation files by failures, then Reviewer-unmet or unclear hunks, then changed lines, and SHALL NOT order them alphabetically (DB-T3).
- **DB-N5-2** WHEN Reviewer findings are shown THE SYSTEM SHALL show the Reviewer's coverage: files not read, and changed lines cited by no finding (DB-T4).
- **DB-N5-3** WHEN any `unmet` or `unclear` finding is unacknowledged, or any Implementation file has not been shown, THE SYSTEM SHALL keep Accept disabled and write which remain beside the button (DB-T5).
- **DB-N5-4** WHEN a card is person-built THE SYSTEM SHALL say so in the outcome line and in Facts (DB-T6).
- **DB-N5-5** WHEN the query `delegate:worker` or `owner:@me` is entered THE SYSTEM SHALL filter by those fields; `assignee:` SHALL mean the owner (DB-T2).
- **DB-N5-6** WHEN a tile is shown THE SYSTEM SHALL show the owner's avatar when the owner is a person and, while a delegate is set, the delegate as a text chip — *Worker*, or the person's name (DB-T1 as ruled by DEC-25 R10).
- **DB-N5-7** WHEN a card supersedes a base test THE SYSTEM SHALL list the old and new test side by side in the Acceptance tests group.
- **DB-N5-8** WHEN the depth profile requires a person's approval of a card's tests THE SYSTEM SHALL show each test's approval state in the Acceptance tests group, and an approval voided by a content change SHALL read *Needs approval again*.
- **DB-N5-9** WHEN independent accept applies and the viewer may not accept THE SYSTEM SHALL disable Accept and name who may accept.

### NEW-dashboard-6 — The Configuration page
*A person cannot see which model does each job, point the harness at the models they have, download a recommended one or compare combinations without a terminal; the chat panel names Seshat's model instead (owner decisions O2 and O3, [DEC-29](../DECISIONS.md#dec-29--the-owners-answers-to-the-decision-queue); the scan and recommendations are [models](models.md) NEW-models-12, the benchmark's mechanics [measurement](measurement.md) NEW-measurement-5).*

- **DB-N6-1** WHEN a person opens `#/configuration`, presses `g c`, or chooses Configuration from the System menu or the palette THE SYSTEM SHALL show the sections Models, Benchmark, Review capacity, This browser and Project configuration; WHEN they open `#/registry` or `#/settings` THE SYSTEM SHALL open Benchmark or This browser respectively.
- **DB-N6-2** WHEN the dashboard is opened and no role's weights are found in any model folder THE SYSTEM SHALL open Configuration › Models instead of the default route; WHEN the Worker role alone has no model that fits THE SYSTEM SHALL keep the default route and show the *No Coding model* bar on every view until it has one.
- **DB-N6-3** WHEN a folder holding two model files is added THE SYSTEM SHALL list both with name, size, quantisation, context length and whether each fits this machine with the reason; WHEN a model does not fit THE SYSTEM SHALL list it with *Needs N GB*; WHEN a folder cannot be read THE SYSTEM SHALL say why beside it and list nothing from it.
- **DB-N6-4** WHEN Models renders THE SYSTEM SHALL show, for Worker, Planner, Reviewer and Researcher in that order, the current model's name, its state, and the recommended model with its reason in one sentence, and the Planner row SHALL say that Seshat runs on it.
- **DB-N6-5** WHEN a person assigns a model that is not qualified for that role on this host ([models](models.md) rule 27a) THE SYSTEM SHALL NOT assign it, SHALL name the missing qualification, and SHALL offer **Qualify to assign**, which runs that check and assigns the model when it passes — it SHALL NOT send the person to Benchmark; WHEN the model is qualified THE SYSTEM SHALL assign it whether or not it has a benchmark score (rule 30a); WHEN the role is Reviewer and the model shares the Worker's family THE SYSTEM SHALL refuse it and say why.
- **DB-N6-6** WHEN no person has pressed Download in its confirmation THE SYSTEM SHALL make no request for weights; WHEN one does THE SYSTEM SHALL have shown the source host, size, published SHA-256, destination folder and what leaves the machine before the download starts, and SHALL mark the model ready only after its hash matches; THE SYSTEM SHALL write only into the folder the confirmation showed, refuse one that is not configured, not there or not writable, never create a missing folder, and never replace a file of the same name.
- **DB-N6-7** WHEN a downloaded file's hash differs from the published one THE SYSTEM SHALL delete the file and say so; WHEN `[network] mode` does not allow the source host THE SYSTEM SHALL disable Download and name the setting.
- **DB-N6-8** WHEN a load would not fit in memory THE SYSTEM SHALL refuse it and say which model would have to be unloaded; WHEN a model a running card uses is unloaded THE SYSTEM SHALL unload it at the next step boundary and say so.
- **DB-N6-9** WHEN a combination is chosen THE SYSTEM SHALL show *Run quick* with its estimate in minutes computed from the roles not yet cached, marking any estimate over its target (a Worker about 17 minutes including its load, each other role about 5, the end-to-end check about 10, a combination with nothing cached about 45), show the sentence *The quick benchmark shows speed, fit and large differences; the overnight benchmark settles close calls*, offer in each role's picker only models that fit (others disabled with *Needs N GB*), and, when one role changes, re-run only that role.
- **DB-N6-10** WHEN a quick or overnight run is in progress THE SYSTEM SHALL show its state (queued, running, stopped or done) and progress, and WHEN a person presses Stop THE SYSTEM SHALL end it and keep every result already measured, marked partial.
- **DB-N6-11** WHEN a person schedules an overnight comparison THE SYSTEM SHALL default to the top combinations the quick tier could not separate, up to 3, say how many fit tonight's window from about 5 hours per combination (with a 22:00–03:00 window and two combinations picked, *Tonight 22:00–03:00 (5 h): 1 of 2 combinations fits*), run only inside the overnight window, stop at its end and resume the next night, and SHALL NOT run while a card runs or the machine is reserved, even when the person is idle inside reserved hours.
- **DB-N6-12** WHEN scores are shown THE SYSTEM SHALL show every pass rate with its exact interval (the overnight tier; the Reviewer's detections), every graded quick-tier score as its mean with the number of items and the range of item scores, and, beside each role's score, its seconds per card, valid tool calls and steps; SHALL label *Indistinguishable*, and rank neither above the other, every two candidates for which the paired sign test on the same items does not reject ([measurement](measurement.md) MS-N5-4) — two Workers where one scores higher on 5 of 6 cards with 1 tie SHALL be *Indistinguishable*, and two whose intervals overlap but where one scores higher on all 6 SHALL be ordered; and after an overnight run SHALL say which differences it resolved and which remain indistinguishable.
- **DB-N6-13** WHEN a benchmark of either tier finishes THE SYSTEM SHALL assign nothing; WHEN a person presses *Assign this combination* on a row whose models are all qualified on this host THE SYSTEM SHALL assign each role and record it with the person's principal; WHEN a row has a model not yet qualified THE SYSTEM SHALL offer *Qualify to assign* in its place.
- **DB-N6-14** WHEN any view shows the chat panel THE SYSTEM SHALL show no model name or id in it, and Configuration SHALL name every role's model, Seshat's included.
- **DB-N6-15** WHEN a person who is not an Admin opens Configuration in the Team setup THE SYSTEM SHALL show Models, Benchmark and Project configuration read-only with that reason beside each control, and Review capacity read-only unless the project's Accept rule names them, and every server change SHALL be recorded with the principal of the person who made it (superseded by DEC-35: the Accept permission no longer decides this); WHEN that person changes theme, density, Learn or first-run role in This browser THE SYSTEM SHALL apply it to their browser, needing no permission and recording nothing on the server.
- **DB-N6-16** WHEN a person chooses *Use the recommended models* THE SYSTEM SHALL show one confirmation listing every download with its source, size and hash and the folder before any request; the confirmation SHALL name back each download's model and hash and the folder, and THE SYSTEM SHALL refuse it, making no request, when they differ from the plan recomputed at that moment; after it THE SYSTEM SHALL download and verify them, run the quick benchmark of the recommended combination, and report which roles were assigned and which were not, with the reason.
- **DB-N6-17** WHEN a non-developer on a machine with no model set up opens the dashboard at 1100 px and at 400 px THE SYSTEM SHALL let them pick the recommended models (or add a suggested folder), run the quick benchmark and read its result with intervals, then schedule an overnight comparison and read the morning report, with no terminal step and no command shown (DEFINITION_OF_DONE §6.4).
- **DB-N6-18** WHEN a role's screen is not built yet (the Reviewer's before B4.8, the Researcher's before B4.4) THE SYSTEM SHALL show *Not measured yet* for that role in the combination builder, Roles and Comparison, SHALL leave it out of every comparison and say so, and SHALL show no score for it ([measurement](measurement.md) MS-N5-4b).
- **DB-N6-19** (FINDINGS CFG-10) WHEN a Benchmark control is disabled THE SYSTEM SHALL say why beside it; WHEN *Schedule overnight* cannot run THE SYSTEM SHALL disable it with the reason before the press; WHEN a model does not fit THE SYSTEM SHALL disable it in every picker with *Needs N GB*; WHEN a person without `config.manage` opens Benchmark THE SYSTEM SHALL show every control disabled with the level note.
- **DB-N6-20** WHEN a person picks settings for a role in the combination builder THE SYSTEM SHALL offer only values a run applies without its own load, name them in words, and estimate, run and list that combination as its own row ([measurement](measurement.md) MS-N8-1).
- **DB-N6-21** WHEN a person uses *Find best settings* THE SYSTEM SHALL show the candidates and the estimate before a confirmation, run nothing before it, show progress with Stop, show the verdict in words (*Best combination* or *No clear difference*), enable **Apply** only for a best or cheaper verdict and apply nothing without that press, and offer *Settle overnight* after no clear difference ([measurement](measurement.md) MS-N7-1..8).
- **DB-N6-22** WHEN Benchmark shows history THE SYSTEM SHALL draw each chart as inline SVG with a table of the same numbers, compare each run with the one before it by the paired test's words, and list the capstone and Web-Bench results with their protocol and validity; at 1440 and 400 px nothing SHALL scroll sideways, and axe SHALL find no WCAG 2.2 A or AA violation.

### NEW-models-3 (the control) — Reserve now on the dashboard
*[models](models.md) rule 20 and [runtime](runtime.md) item 17 reserve the machine when a person presses *Reserve now*, but no spec owned the control or tested it (final check m-h). The change is [models](models.md)' NEW-models-3; this spec carries only the control (§2.11), [surface](surface.md) the commands (SUR-54, SUR-55), and MD-N3-1 and MD-N3-4 test their effect.*

- **DB-NM3-1** WHEN a person presses *Reserve now* on Machine, or chooses *Reserve the machine* in the palette, THE SYSTEM SHALL record the reservation with the person's principal, show *Reserved by you since HH:MM* with a *Release* button, start no backlog card unattended unless it is urgent, and start or continue no overnight benchmark until a release.
- **DB-NM3-2** WHEN a person presses *Release*, or `sekhemet dev release` runs, THE SYSTEM SHALL record the release with the person's principal and show the machine as free, or in its reserved hours, within one machine update (5 s), and SHALL NOT change `[machine] reserved_hours`; WHEN `sekhemet dev reserve` runs THE SYSTEM SHALL show the machine as reserved on Machine within the same interval.

### NEW-dashboard-7 — Professional language on every screen ([DEC-31](../DECISIONS.md#dec-31))

*How (B4.7 G0): a card's issue type is derived, never stored — `issueTypeOf` in `vocabulary.ts` maps an epic or initiative to Epic, a spike or research card to Spike, a fix to Bug, a refactor, upgrade, characterization, review card or `task`-tier card to Task, and the rest to Story. Estimation is a project setting on the ledger (`project/settings_changed` `{ estimation: "off" | "points" }`, TEAM-32's event), changed from Configuration › Preferences by the project's lead or an Admin (`project.settings`) and served on `/api/board`; the burn-up counts issues with `?unit=issues`. Internal identifiers, event names and API fields keep their names (`cycle`, `gates`, `worker`, `kind`); only what a person reads changes. The test DB-N7-1 names is `packages/ui/tests/professional_language.spec.ts`; DB-N7-4's is `apps/harness/tests/cli_language.spec.ts`, and both read literals through one scan (`packages/ui/tests/copy_scan.ts`: every string literal, comments and regex literals left out, identifiers — interpolations, attributes, routes, event types, file names, `sekhemet <command>`, `--flags`, hyphenated and underscored names — stripped, then DEC-31's retired words, *card* among them unless it is the board's (*board card*)).*

- **DB-N7-1** WHEN any view renders THE SYSTEM SHALL use the on-screen terms of DEC-31's table (issue, Story/Task/Bug/Spike/Epic, checks, sprint, release, Agent, Coding/Planning/Review/Research model, Tips, Preferences), and a test SHALL fail on any retired display label (*Contract*, *Storage*, *Flow*, *Rules* as kinds; *gates*, *must-haves proven*, *walking skeleton* outside Tips) in the page's copy.
- **DB-N7-2** WHEN estimation is off (the default) THE SYSTEM SHALL show no points on cards, columns or reports; WHEN a team turns on story points THE SYSTEM SHALL show them as Jira does.
- **DB-N7-3** WHEN a card is drawn on the board THE SYSTEM SHALL show its key, issue type icon, priority, title, one status line, epic and assignee, and no step counter or model name.
- **DB-N7-4** WHEN the CLI prints, the server returns a message a page shows, a notification is sent, or Sekhemet writes a report, pull request body or project document THE SYSTEM SHALL use DEC-31's words — *issue* everywhere but the tile on a board, which copy names *board card*; *checks*; *Agent* and the *Coding*, *Planning*, *Review* and *Research model*; *release*; *verified on this machine*; *no clear difference* — while command names, flags, event types, routes, API fields and stored values keep their internal names; and a test SHALL fail on any retired word in those modules' literals, the literals a model reads as its prompt excepted and named.

### NEW-dashboard-8 — The issue page: working with the agent ([DEC-34](../DECISIONS.md#dec-34))

- **DB-N8-1** WHEN an issue page is open THE SYSTEM SHALL show its description, acceptance criteria with each one's check state, and tabs *Activity*, *Checks*, *Changes* and *AI review*; Activity SHALL interleave the agent's plan, progress, questions and the people's messages in time order.
- **DB-N8-2** WHEN the agent is running THE SYSTEM SHALL offer a message box labelled for the agent ("Message the agent — it reads this at its next step"), *Pause*, and *Take over*; WHEN it is paused or taken over THE SYSTEM SHALL offer *Hand back* with a note.
- **DB-N8-3** WHEN the agent has asked a question THE SYSTEM SHALL show it in Activity with its options as buttons, its default, and whether the agent is continuing on the default or waiting.
- **DB-N8-4** WHEN a person reviews the Changes tab THE SYSTEM SHALL let them comment on a diff line, and *Request changes* (DEC-52; *Send back* before it) SHALL carry those comments.

### NEW-dashboard-9 — The pages for working together ([DEC-35](../DECISIONS.md#dec-35--one-product-two-setups-solo-and-team), [DEC-37](../DECISIONS.md#dec-37--status-serves-the-stakeholder-and-the-team-from-the-same-data))
*Status is shallow for a stakeholder and for the team; a team has no sign-in, no Projects page, no inbox and no Admin pages; the brand mark and AI identities are not drawn (owner, 2026-09-25). Behaviour is [teams](teams.md)'s; these criteria are the page's.*

- **DB-N9-1** WHEN Status renders THE SYSTEM SHALL show, in §2.8's order, the headline, health with the name of the person who set it and the date, the latest update, the key-number strip, the burn-up with its scope line, forecast band and target line, Needs you, Waiting on others with each item's owner, requirements by MoSCoW group and state, risks, Done this week with who accepted each issue, Who's working on what, the models line and the flow strip.
- **DB-N9-2** WHEN, in the Team setup, no person has set a project's health THE SYSTEM SHALL show *No health set*, and SHALL offer *Set health* only to the project lead or a Member who leads a release ([teams](teams.md) TEAM-28).
- **DB-N9-3** WHEN a forecast is shown on Status or Projects THE SYSTEM SHALL show its 50% and 85% dates and the target, and never a single date; WHEN the finished-issue history is below the minimum THE SYSTEM SHALL show *Not enough history yet*.
- **DB-N9-4** WHEN a person presses *Write update* THE SYSTEM SHALL open Seshat's draft in five parts (status, done, next, risks, asks) in an editor and post nothing until that person presses *Post*; WHEN it is posted THE SYSTEM SHALL show it under the headline with its author and date; WHEN, in the Team setup, 7 days pass since the last posted update, or since the release started if none was posted, THE SYSTEM SHALL show *Update missing* to the lead ([teams](teams.md) TEAM-29).
- **DB-N9-5** WHEN requirements are listed on Status THE SYSTEM SHALL group them as Must have, Should have and Could have, show each as done, in progress, tests too weak, blocked or not started, and SHALL NOT count one whose tests are too weak as done.
- **DB-N9-6** WHEN a risk is shown THE SYSTEM SHALL show it as a sentence followed by *Suggested: … Why: …*, and SHALL change nothing until a person applies the suggestion.
- **DB-N9-7** WHEN Status, Projects or Insights renders THE SYSTEM SHALL show no per-person count, rate, velocity or ranking and no comparison of the Agent with people; Who's working on what SHALL show only each person's and the Agent's current item.
- **DB-N9-8** WHEN the flow strip renders THE SYSTEM SHALL show median and 85th-percentile cycle time, throughput, issues sent back and the checks' first-time pass rate, each linking to Insights.
- **DB-N9-9** WHEN a person opens Projects THE SYSTEM SHALL show the totals (waiting on you, active projects, shipped this month, Agent time today), one row per project they can see with health, current release and progress, forecast range and target, what waits on them, what the Agent is doing and the lead, the Waiting on you list, and each model on the server with its load and memory; WHEN `#/workspace` is opened THE SYSTEM SHALL open Projects.
- **DB-N9-10** WHEN the sidebar renders THE SYSTEM SHALL show, top to bottom, the brand and Search; Projects, Inbox and (Team only) My issues; the project switcher, Status, Board, Review, Project manager, Insights and More; and at the bottom the Agent status line, Configuration and the account.
- **DB-N9-11** WHEN the account menu opens THE SYSTEM SHALL show the person's name and email (in Solo, the name and *This computer*), Profile, Notifications, Keyboard shortcuts and Theme with *System*, *Light* and *Dark*, and in the Team setup also Members and Sign out, and in both setups Switch workspace (*amended by DEC-57*: Switch workspace was Team only); WHEN *System* is chosen THE SYSTEM SHALL follow `prefers-color-scheme`.
- **DB-N9-12** WHEN Sekhemet runs in Solo THE SYSTEM SHALL show no sign-in page, no My issues, and no Members, Audit or Sign out. *(Amended by DEC-57, the later ruling: Solo shows Switch workspace, DB-N25-3.)*
- **DB-N9-13** WHEN a person opens Sign in in the Team setup THE SYSTEM SHALL show email and password, the company SSO and passkey buttons only when each is enabled, *Ask an admin for an invite link*, and the footer *Self-hosted · your code and data stay on this server*, and SHALL show no sign-up form unless an Admin opened sign-up for a domain.
- **DB-N9-14** WHEN the Inbox renders THE SYSTEM SHALL group its items as Needs you, Mentioned, Review requested, Watching and Agent finished, offer Done, Snooze and Save on each, and show an AI teammate's current state on items about its work.
- **DB-N9-15** WHEN a person opens My issues THE SYSTEM SHALL list the issues they own, are delegated or are asked to review, across the projects they can see, grouped by project.
- **DB-N9-16** WHEN an Admin opens Members THE SYSTEM SHALL list each person with level, per-project overrides, profile label and last active, and offer Invite, level changes, overrides, label changes and removal; WHEN anyone else opens it THE SYSTEM SHALL show the table without those actions; WHEN anyone but an Admin opens Audit THE SYSTEM SHALL refuse the page and say that an Admin can open it.
- **DB-N9-17** WHEN a control is not allowed at the viewer's access level THE SYSTEM SHALL disable it and write beside it the level the viewer holds and a level that can do it.
- **DB-N9-18** WHEN Seshat or the Agent is named on any page THE SYSTEM SHALL show the *AI* badge beside the name, SHALL give the Agent no avatar, and SHALL NOT show the badge beside a person.
- **DB-N9-19** WHEN the brand mark renders THE SYSTEM SHALL draw the pylons in the theme's ink and the disc in the theme's gold, at no less than 16 px (80 px for the lockup), and SHALL NOT draw it recoloured or on a gold ground.
- **DB-N9-20** WHEN two people view the same issue THE SYSTEM SHALL show each other's avatar in its header, and a card someone is dragging SHALL show that person's avatar ([teams](teams.md) TEAM-26).
- **DB-N9-21** WHEN a person opens Projects THE SYSTEM SHALL offer *New project*, which opens the start-project conversation; WHEN there are no projects THE SYSTEM SHALL show only *Start your first project* with a button that opens it; WHEN Sekhemet runs in Solo THE SYSTEM SHALL show neither *No health set* nor *Update missing* on any page ([teams](teams.md) TEAM-45).

### NEW-models-14 (the view) — model details, combinations and placement
*[models](models.md) owns Smart Swap (NEW-models-14, rules 20d–20k, [DEC-45](../DECISIONS.md#dec-45--smart-swap)) and the measurement of placement is [measurement](measurement.md)'s (rule 16d); this spec carries the page (§2.16 item 1a). Workstream **B4.1**. Each criterion is tested on a server with fixture ledgers, a fake memory probe and fake benchmark results, loading no model.*

- **DB-NM14-1** WHEN the model section renders any number THE SYSTEM SHALL show beside it its grade, *Measured*, *From the file*, *Estimated* or *Design value* — a value read from the model's file (its size, its header) is *From the file*, never *Measured*, and an unknown size is said so, never shown as 0; a test rendering one of each finds each labelled, and a number without a grade fails the test.
- **DB-NM14-2** WHEN a person opens a model THE SYSTEM SHALL show its identity (family, total and active parameters, quantisation, engine, size, maximum context, licence, source, hash) and its memory breakdown (weights, KV, compute buffer, prompt cache) against the live headroom; WHEN they change the context size or KV type in the what-if THE SYSTEM SHALL recompute the breakdown and load nothing.
- **DB-NM14-3** WHEN speeds are shown THE SYSTEM SHALL show the efficiency estimate as *Estimated* until a llama-bench run of one warm-up and five runs at the role's depth has a spread of 3% or less, then that result as *Measured*; a run with a larger spread SHALL be shown as not accepted and not used; llama-bench SHALL run only on an Admin's **Measure speed** confirmed by the model's name and the memory it loads, inside one benchmark run of the residency scheduler (no other role loads meanwhile; only what it loaded is unloaded), recorded as `model/speed_measured` with the principal; and THE SYSTEM SHALL show time to first token with and without the prefix cache and cold and warm load time per volume. Because llama-bench, like a qualification from the page, loads the weights where the residency scheduler cannot see them, the run first unloads every idle resident model and refuses while one is in use (`BenchmarkLease.exclusive()`).
- **DB-NM14-4** WHEN a model has a warning (won't fit, slow drive, unqualified for a role, licence, Ollama requantisation, an aging cap below C_pair) THE SYSTEM SHALL state it in words, and for an infeasible cap SHALL show the predicted wait instead ([models](models.md) MD-N14-20); per role it SHALL show the qualification status of the full combination.
- **DB-NM14-5** WHEN a person asks for a pre-download estimate of a remote model THE SYSTEM SHALL read only its GGUF metadata through its own estimator over `@huggingface/gguf` and the `[network]` policy's fetch, refuse a host that ignores Range (anything but a 206) and stop past a byte cap, label the result *Estimated*, and fetch no weights (a test observes only metadata range requests); gguf-parser-go SHALL be given a local file only (`--path`), never its `--url`, which bypasses the `[network]` policy.
- **DB-NM14-6** WHEN combinations are shown THE SYSTEM SHALL exclude, with the reason, any that breaks the family rule or does not fit; SHALL show for each the peak memory, the co-residence, C_pair per role pair, predicted wait p50 and p90 per class (Seshat's included), time per card (*Estimated* while fewer than 20 of the person's cards were replayed) and accepted cards per night; SHALL order them by quality floors, then time per card, then footprint; and SHALL show no combined score.
- **DB-NM14-7** WHEN placement is advised THE SYSTEM SHALL show, per model on a slower volume, the swaps' loading time saved per day, suggest copies as a knapsack that keeps at least 20 GB of internal space free, and say why a copy that would break that is not suggested.
- **DB-NM14-8** WHEN no person has pressed **Copy** in the confirmation (showing size, destination, free space after, and hash) THE SYSTEM SHALL copy nothing; WHEN one does THE SYSTEM SHALL verify the copy's SHA-256 before pointing the registry at it, record the copy on the ledger with the person's principal, in an event registered as structural only ([models](models.md) MD-N14-40a), and keep the original; WHEN the hash differs THE SYSTEM SHALL delete the copy, leave the registry unchanged and say so.
- **DB-NM14-9** WHEN the section renders THE SYSTEM SHALL show a 24-hour residency timeline built from the recorded `model/loaded` and `model/unloaded` events with the realised θ per hour, an engine comparison for each model found in both llama.cpp and MLX builds, and every stored benchmark run of either kind with its date and settings.

### NEW-dashboard-10 — Intake: Stakeholders file and Members triage
*[teams](teams.md) grants Stakeholders "file issues", but New issue checks `issue.create` (a Member's); and issues filed by Stakeholders, integrations or imports land in Backlog with no triage step, which Linear and Jira teams expect (FINDINGS PRC-01; DESIGN_GAPS b4; v1 by DEC-51). No new state and no new permission (§2.4.19). Owner workflow C2.*

- **DB-N10-1** WHEN a Stakeholder chooses *New issue* or presses `c` THE SYSTEM SHALL open the same form and file the issue through `issue.file`, with the project lead as its assignee.
- **DB-N10-2** WHERE a Backlog issue was filed by a person below Member, by an integration or by an import, and no Member has triaged it, THE SYSTEM SHALL list it in the built-in *Triage* view.
- **DB-N10-3** WHEN a Member chooses *Accept into Backlog*, *Decline* (Won't do, with a reason), *Duplicate of* or *Snooze* on a Triage row (keys `1`–`3` and `H`) THE SYSTEM SHALL record `issue/triaged {decision}`, remove the row, and record no `card/accepted`; Seshat's existing triage suggestions SHALL show beside each row as proposals that only a person applies.
- **DB-N10-4** WHEN Triage holds rows THE SYSTEM SHALL show their count in the project lead's Inbox under *Needs you*; WHERE the setup is Solo THE SYSTEM SHALL hide the Triage view.

### NEW-dashboard-11 — The sprint lifecycle
*Sprints exist, but the product has no Start or Complete sprint; unfinished issues are not carried over; there is no sprint report; with no sprint the toast tells a person to "create one with POST /api/cycles"; and [planner-pm](planner-pm.md) §2.7 cites carry-over history that nothing records. Jira and Linear teams use this every two weeks (FINDINGS PRC-02; DESIGN_GAPS b6; v1 by DEC-51; §2.4.13, §2.4.20). Owner workflow C2.*

- **DB-N11-1** WHEN a Member chooses *Start sprint* on a planned sprint THE SYSTEM SHALL record the start with the committed issues, and their points when estimation is on; WHEN another sprint of the project is active THE SYSTEM SHALL refuse the start and name the active sprint.
- **DB-N11-2** WHEN a Member chooses *Complete sprint* THE SYSTEM SHALL list the done and not-done issues and ask where the not-done ones go — the next planned sprint, a new sprint, or Backlog — and SHALL apply the moves and the close as one recorded group.
- **DB-N11-3** WHEN a sprint completes THE SYSTEM SHALL show a sprint report computed from the ledger: committed at start, added, removed, completed and carried over.
- **DB-N11-4** WHEN Seshat judges a sprint due to close THE SYSTEM SHALL offer a `close_cycle` proposal that a person applies, and SHALL NOT close a sprint without that person's press (DEC-36).
- **DB-N11-5** WHEN an action needs a sprint and none exists THE SYSTEM SHALL offer *New sprint* and *Plan a sprint with Seshat*, and SHALL show no API path.

### NEW-dashboard-12 — Full-text search
*The palette and the query box match only title and key over the loaded project (FINDINGS PRC-04; DESIGN_GAPS b14; v1 by DEC-51, which names SQLite FTS5 in the built-in `node:sqlite` and no library; §2.4.12, §2.4.21). Owner workflow C2.*

- **DB-N12-1** WHEN a person types free words in the palette or the query box THE SYSTEM SHALL match title, key, description, acceptance criteria and comment text, including Done and Won't do issues, across every project the person can see (`GET /api/search?q=`).
- **DB-N12-2** WHEN a search runs THE SYSTEM SHALL return only what the person's visibility and level allow, and SHALL NOT return text that a `ledger/erased` event erased.
- **DB-N12-3** WHEN the W9 seeded board holds 10,000 issues THE SYSTEM SHALL answer a search within the interaction budget (p75 ≤ 200 ms).

### NEW-dashboard-13 — Lessons for the practice a person performs
*"Juniors learn the real practice" (the owner's direction); today Tips teach board terms and flow metrics, not the practices a person performs (FINDINGS PRC-05; DESIGN_GAPS b15; §2.9.5). The Learn sheet (§2.9.3) is not part of this change. Owner workflow C2.*

- **DB-N13-1** WHERE Tips are on THE SYSTEM SHALL offer lessons on reviewing a change, requesting changes with a useful note, approving acceptance criteria (at the criteria-approval hold), priority and MoSCoW, blocked work and dependencies, the Definition of done, triage, sprint planning and the retrospective.
- **DB-N13-2** WHEN a lesson renders THE SYSTEM SHALL show one line computed from the project's own numbers and one canonical link (the Scrum Guide, Atlassian's or Linear's documentation), in DEC-31's and DEC-52's words.
- **DB-N13-3** WHEN a lesson's subject first appears on screen for a learner THE SYSTEM SHALL offer the lesson once, and SHALL NOT block the action.

### NEW-dashboard-14 — The Definition of done and readiness, readable
*The team's Definition of done and the entry conditions are enforced but stated nowhere a team or a junior can read (FINDINGS PRC-12; DESIGN_GAPS b19; §2.16.5, §2.6). Owner workflow C2.*

- **DB-N14-1** WHEN a person opens Configuration › Project THE SYSTEM SHALL show a read-only *Definition of done* in plain words, built from `gates.toml`, the depth profile and the Accept rule.
- **DB-N14-2** WHEN an issue is in Backlog or To do THE SYSTEM SHALL show *Ready to start* on its page, listing each kernel entry condition as met or not met, with the reason.
- **DB-N14-3** WHEN the Definition of done or *Ready to start* is shown THE SYSTEM SHALL enforce nothing new and loosen no check: a test SHALL find the same transitions allowed and refused with and without them.

### NEW-dashboard-15 — New issue by type, with a Bug's reproduction
*A fix card needs "a defect with a reproduction" ([planner-pm](planner-pm.md) §2.16), but quick create asks only for a title and a description (FINDINGS BRD-05; DESIGN_GAPS b20; §2.4.22). Seshat's question for the Bug fields is model-facing and follows [PROMPT_STANDARD](../PROMPT_STANDARD.md) rule 35. Owner workflow C2.*

- **DB-N15-1** WHEN a person chooses *New issue* THE SYSTEM SHALL offer type, priority, labels, sprint and assignee as property pills, in DEC-31's type words.
- **DB-N15-2** WHEN the type is Bug THE SYSTEM SHALL ask for *What happened*, *What you expected*, *Steps* and *Release* (the tagged releases), each optional, and the planner SHALL use them as the fix card's reproduction input; WHEN a person tells Seshat that something is broken, Seshat SHALL ask for the same fields.
- **DB-N15-3** WHERE the repository has `.github/ISSUE_TEMPLATE` issue forms THE SYSTEM SHALL use their fields for the matching type, and SHALL pass their text to no model as instructions.

### NEW-dashboard-16 — Undo for field and bulk edits
*Field edits, moves, holds, Won't do and bulk edits cannot be undone; the only undo is Accept's 3-second grace, and it is untested (FINDINGS BRD-07; DESIGN_GAPS b21; §2.4.23). Owner workflow C2.*

- **DB-N16-1** WHEN an inline or bulk field edit, a move, a hold or a Won't do is applied THE SYSTEM SHALL show a result toast offering *Undo* (`z`) for 10 s.
- **DB-N16-2** WHEN *Undo* is chosen THE SYSTEM SHALL append compensating events that restore the previous values, leaving every earlier event in the log, and say what it restored; WHEN another person changed a field since THE SYSTEM SHALL leave that field as it is and name it.
- **DB-N16-3** WHEN an issue is accepted THE SYSTEM SHALL offer only Accept's own grace window and revert (review-git S5), and SHALL NOT offer this undo.
- **DB-N16-4** (C4; FINDINGS REL-08, unhappy UH-4) WHEN an issue's fields are edited THE SYSTEM SHALL give the issue a version — a digest of its editable fields (`cardVersion`, the same function on the server and the page), answered as the `ETag` of `GET` and `PATCH /api/cards/:id` — and the page SHALL send the version of the issue it shows as `If-Match`; WHEN that version is not the issue's current one THE SYSTEM SHALL apply nothing and answer 409 with the current values, the current version and who changed it last, and the page SHALL show the current value on the issue and say the change was not applied. A `PATCH` without `If-Match` (a script, the MCP tool) is applied as before. A change to a field no person edits (a run's step count) does not change the version.

### NEW-dashboard-17 — Accepting an issue without reading a diff
*In Solo, a non-developer's *Review it* opens the diff-first Review page, whose audience is developers (FINDINGS REV-10; DESIGN_GAPS b22; §2.6 *Criteria view*). Nothing about Accept changes but the page it starts from. Owner workflow C2.*

- **DB-N17-1** WHEN a person whose first-run route is *I manage the work*, or in the Team setup whose profile label is Product owner or Stakeholder, chooses *Review it* THE SYSTEM SHALL open the issue's criteria view: each acceptance criterion in plain words with its check state, the AI review's summary and the visual check's screenshots, with *Accept* and *Request changes*, and the diff one tab away.
- **DB-N17-2** WHEN Accept is pressed in the criteria view THE SYSTEM SHALL record the same Accept event, under the same gates, Accept rule and enabling conditions (§2.5.9) as Review does; WHEN a condition is not met THE SYSTEM SHALL keep Accept disabled and write the condition beside it.

### NEW-dashboard-18 — Status as a grid at wide widths
*§2.8 fixes the order of Status, not its layout; the approved Status mockup is a dashboard grid, and the build is one 960 px column (FINDINGS STA-08; DESIGN_GAPS b24; adopted by DEC-51; §2.8.14). Owner workflow C2.*

- **DB-N18-1** WHERE the window is 1280 px or wider THE SYSTEM SHALL lay Status out as the mockup's grid — the key-number strip first; the burn-up (two thirds) beside *Needs you* and *Waiting on others* (one third); a three-column band of Requirements, Risks and Who's working above Flow — and SHALL keep §2.8's reading order.
- **DB-N18-2** WHERE the window is narrower than 768 px THE SYSTEM SHALL keep one column (§2.8.13).

### NEW-dashboard-19 — The approved mockups where they are more complete ([DEC-51](../DECISIONS.md#dec-51--c1s-k3-list-decided))
*DEC-51 rules that the approved `dashboard-v3` mockups win where they are more complete than the spec: the Issue page's properties rail (FINDINGS ISS-01), Members' Invites and access parts (TEAM-04), Configuration's cards (CFG-07), the brand mark as drawn in `Logo.dc.html` (VIS-02), and the Inbox in two panes at 1100 px and wider, one list on a phone (TEAM-03). §2.6, §2.17.4, §2.16, §2.13.6 and §2.17.2 are amended. Owner workflow C2.*

- **DB-N19-1** WHEN an issue page opens at 1280 px or wider THE SYSTEM SHALL show a 288 px properties rail with Assignee, Delegate, Reviewers, Reporter, Type, Priority, Points (estimation on only), Sprint, Epic, Release, Labels, Due, Blocked by, Blocks, Branch and Watchers; WHEN it opens from 1024 to 1279 px THE SYSTEM SHALL show them in a *Properties* disclosure under the header.
- **DB-N19-2** WHEN a person edits a rail field THE SYSTEM SHALL use the list's editor for that field and record the same event as a list edit; WHEN the viewer's level does not allow the change THE SYSTEM SHALL show the field read-only with the level note beside it.
- **DB-N19-3** WHEN Members renders THE SYSTEM SHALL show the tabs Members, Invites (an Admin only) and Sign-in, the columns Name, Access, Labels, Projects and *Can accept in*, and the AI teammates and Access levels parts, the latter from the same table the server checks.
- **DB-N19-4** WHEN an Admin opens Invites THE SYSTEM SHALL list every outstanding invite with its email, level, project, sender and expiry, and *Revoke* SHALL make the link unusable at once; WHEN anyone else asks for the invites THE SYSTEM SHALL refuse.
- **DB-N19-5** WHEN Configuration renders THE SYSTEM SHALL show the machine line under the title, the sections as one underlined tab bar, and Models' parts as the cards Model library, Available models, Suggested setup and Compare setups; WHEN *Apply suggestion* is pressed THE SYSTEM SHALL open the same single confirmation as *Use the recommended models* (DB-N6-16) and change nothing before it.
- **DB-N19-6** WHEN the brand mark, the favicon or the app icon renders THE SYSTEM SHALL centre the disc at (12, 6.5) on a 24-unit square, on the line of the pylons' tops and between them, with radius 2.4; a test SHALL fail on a disc lower in the gate.
- **DB-N19-7** WHEN the Inbox renders in a window 1100 px or wider THE SYSTEM SHALL show the grouped list and a reading pane for the selected item, with Snooze, Save, Done, the issue's column, assignee, delegate and release, what caused the item, a reply box with *Reply*, and *Open issue*.
- **DB-N19-8** WHEN a person selects an Inbox item with a click or `j/k` THE SYSTEM SHALL fill the reading pane, move focus with the selection, and keep the person in the Inbox; WHEN they press *Reply* THE SYSTEM SHALL post the reply on the issue as their comment.
- **DB-N19-9** WHEN the Inbox renders in a window narrower than 1100 px THE SYSTEM SHALL show one list, and opening an item SHALL show its reading pane in place of the list with *Back to Inbox*, which returns focus to the same row.

### NEW-dashboard-20 — Primary buttons in dark ink; gold as the brand accent ([DEC-51](../DECISIONS.md#dec-51--c1s-k3-list-decided))
*The spec contradicted itself on the primary button (§2.1.4 and §2.13.7 said gold; §2.13's standard drew the dark button) and the build uses gold everywhere while every mockup draws ink (FINDINGS SPEC-07, from domain17: "primary action" and "needs attention" looked alike). DEC-51 rules dark ink, with gold the brand accent and the warning hue its own. Owner workflow C2.*

- **DB-N20-1** WHEN any primary button renders, in either theme, THE SYSTEM SHALL fill it with `--text-primary` and set its text in `--on-ink`, and no button SHALL use `--accent` as its fill.
- **DB-N20-2** WHEN the page is scanned for uses of `--accent` THE SYSTEM SHALL find them only in the active-nav indicator, the focus ring and the brand mark's disc.
- **DB-N20-3** WHEN the token test runs THE SYSTEM SHALL assert `--on-ink` on `--text-primary` at 4.5:1 or more in both themes, and SHALL find every warning treatment (a wait past its limit, a sprint behind pace, a capacity bar at its limit) in `--state-parked`, at least 20° of hue from `--accent` (DB-P12-2).

### NEW-dashboard-21 — Won't do, Reopen and Revert in the dashboard ([DEC-51](../DECISIONS.md#dec-51--c1s-k3-list-decided))
*Won't do, Reopen and Revert exist only on the command line: no dashboard control, no reopen route, no HTTP test for reject or revert (FINDINGS ISS-04; v1 by DEC-51; §2.6 *Issue actions*). The transitions are [review-git](review-git.md)'s (RG-S5-10, RG-S5-11). Owner workflow C2.*

- **DB-N21-1** WHEN a person chooses *Won't do* on an issue in Backlog, To do, In review or On hold THE SYSTEM SHALL require a reason and move the issue to *Won't do*; WHEN the issue is in any other column THE SYSTEM SHALL NOT offer the action.
- **DB-N21-2** WHEN a person chooses *Reopen* on a *Won't do* issue THE SYSTEM SHALL move it to To do through `POST /api/cards/:id/reopen`, tested over HTTP with the reject and revert routes.
- **DB-N21-3** WHEN a person the project's Accept rule names chooses *Revert* on a Done issue and confirms the dialog that names the commit THE SYSTEM SHALL add a revert commit of its squash to the integration branch, move the issue to To do and record `card/reverted` with both shas; WHEN the Accept rule does not name the person THE SYSTEM SHALL disable *Revert* and say who may revert.
- **DB-N21-4** WHEN git cannot apply a revert THE SYSTEM SHALL leave the issue in Done, add no commit, and show the server's reason with the files it names.

### NEW-dashboard-22 — A notification when work waits ([DEC-53](../DECISIONS.md#dec-53--design_gaps-c-decided) c4)
*"The human is the rate limiter": a Solo person with no ntfy, Gotify, Slack or SMTP is never told that an issue waits in Review (FINDINGS PRC-11). DEC-53 c4 rules a browser notification from the open dashboard, the permission asked once, with no library; and, as the same row's smallest proposal, an operating-system notification through `osascript` or `notify-send` only when the dashboard is closed (§2.16.4). *D1 review fix: the first draft moved the operating-system notification to §7 citing DEC-53, which rules no such deferral; DB-N22-5 to -7 restore it.* Owner workflow C2.*

- **DB-N22-1** WHEN no person in this browser has pressed *Turn on* THE SYSTEM SHALL make no notification permission request; WHEN a person presses *Not now* on the one-time offer THE SYSTEM SHALL NOT show the offer again in that browser.
- **DB-N22-2** WHEN notifications are on, a dashboard tab is open but not in front, and an issue enters In review (in the Team setup, an issue reaches the person's Inbox under *Needs you* or *Review requested*) THE SYSTEM SHALL raise one notification naming the issue's key, title and where it waits, with no code, diff or model text; WHEN the person clicks it THE SYSTEM SHALL bring the tab to the front and open the issue in Review.
- **DB-N22-3** WHEN several such events arrive within 60 seconds THE SYSTEM SHALL raise one notification that counts them.
- **DB-N22-4** WHEN the browser has blocked notifications for the dashboard THE SYSTEM SHALL say so in Preferences, with how to allow them, and SHALL raise none.
- **DB-N22-5** WHEN *Also notify me on this computer* is on in the Solo setup, no dashboard tab holds the live stream, and an issue enters In review THE SYSTEM SHALL raise one operating-system notification through `osascript` on macOS or `notify-send` on Linux, naming the issue's key, title and where it waits, with no code, diff or model text, batched as DB-N22-3 says; WHEN a dashboard tab holds the live stream THE SYSTEM SHALL raise none.
- **DB-N22-6** WHEN the operating-system notification is raised THE SYSTEM SHALL pass the issue's text to the tool as an argument with no shell, so that a title holding quotes, `$(…)` or AppleScript runs nothing (a test raises one for such a title against a stub tool and checks its argument list).
- **DB-N22-7** WHEN neither tool is present, or the setup is Team, THE SYSTEM SHALL disable or hide the switch with its reason and raise no operating-system notification; the setting SHALL be off until a person turns it on.

### NEW-dashboard-23 — The professional words on screen ([DEC-52](../DECISIONS.md#dec-52--the-professional-words-naming-amended))
*DEC-52 amends NAMING and DEC-31's table with the industry's words (§2.12.5); the code keeps its internal names and the CLI its aliases. Where DEC-25 R10, DEC-31 and NAMING row 22 used *owner* on screen and *assignee* for the agent, DEC-52, the later ruling, wins. Owner workflow C2, in the rename pass DEC-52 assigns it. FINDINGS TEAM-01, WRD-03.*

- **DB-N23-1** WHEN any view names the person responsible for an issue THE SYSTEM SHALL label the field *Assignee*, and the AI teammate or person building it *Delegate*; WHEN a person types `assignee:` or `owner:` in the query box THE SYSTEM SHALL filter by the same stored field.
- **DB-N23-2** WHEN the triage bar, the bulk bar, the cheat sheet, the palette, a menu or a toast names these actions THE SYSTEM SHALL say *Request changes*, *Put on hold* and *Take off hold*, and the keys `r` and `p` SHALL keep their actions.
- **DB-N23-3** WHEN the event log is named on screen — the nav item, the view's title, the shell bar, the Agent status line, the issue's `⋯` menu and Activity — THE SYSTEM SHALL call it *Activity log*; event types in mono SHALL stay as recorded.
- **DB-N23-4** WHEN requirement, criterion or scope terms render THE SYSTEM SHALL say *Needs re-checking*, *Tests too weak*, *Acceptance criteria* and *Files in scope*.
- **DB-N23-5** WHEN the page shows a refusal or error that names an issue's state THE SYSTEM SHALL name its board column (*In review*, *To do*, *Done*); a test over every refusal the server can return to the page SHALL fail on a stored state id (`verify`, `ready`, `parked`, `rejected`) in its text.
- **DB-N23-6** WHEN the professional-language test (DB-N7-1) runs THE SYSTEM SHALL also fail on the retired on-screen words *owner* (as a field label), *Send back*, *Park*, *Unpark*, *Ledger*, *Suspect*, *Passing, strength unmet*, *Done when* and *May edit*, outside Pipeline stages, mono event types and the CLI.

### NEW-dashboard-24 — Network activity in Project configuration ([security](security.md) NEW-security-11)
*Justification: egress is recorded (security item 33) but never shown, and professional security reviews ask for every outbound connection. Security item 33a defines the view's content and its CLI twin; this change places it in the page (§2.16 item 5), since Configuration's tabs are fixed by the approved mockup (NEW-dashboard-19). [DESIGN_GAPS_C1](../../reference/DESIGN_GAPS_C1.md) b17, the dashboard half; FINDINGS INS-08. Owner workflow C2 (the view); C5 writes the guide page it links.*

- **DB-N24-1** WHEN a person opens Configuration › Project THE SYSTEM SHALL show the *Network activity* card last, listing the project's recorded `harness/egress`, `card/egress` and `model/downloaded` events newest first, each with time, host, purpose, *Allowed* or *Refused*, size, and the issue or person that caused it — the same rows `sekhemet egress` prints for the same ledger (SEC-N11-2).
- **DB-N24-2** WHEN no such event is recorded THE SYSTEM SHALL show *Nothing has left this machine.*; WHEN *Since* or *Refused only* is set THE SYSTEM SHALL show only the matching rows.
- **DB-N24-3** WHEN the card renders THE SYSTEM SHALL record no event, offer no action, show each person only rows their access level can read, and show an erased private part as erased.

### NEW-dashboard-25 — The project switcher and the workspace switcher ([DEC-57](../DECISIONS.md#dec-57--a-server-is-a-workspace-with-many-projects))
*Justification: the sidebar prints the repository's absolute path where the approved mockups draw a project switcher, so with no chooser Configuration's "choose a project in the sidebar" leads nowhere and project settings stay unreachable (FINDINGS SHL-01, severity 3). DEC-57 makes a server a workspace of many projects and adds a workspace switcher that lists the other workspaces this person has used on this machine. §2.2 item 7 places both where the mockups put them: the project switcher heads the Project group, and *Switch workspace* is in the account menu. Owner workflow **C2a**.*

- **DB-N25-1** WHEN the sidebar renders THE SYSTEM SHALL show the project switcher with the current project's initials badge and name, and SHALL NOT show the repository's path in the sidebar; the path SHALL appear in the switcher's tooltip and in Configuration › Project (SHL-01).
- **DB-N25-2** WHEN the project switcher opens THE SYSTEM SHALL list every project `GET /api/projects/overview` returns for the person, filtered as they type, with *New project* at its foot only for a person who may create a project; WHEN one is chosen THE SYSTEM SHALL make it the current project of every project page, stay on the same kind of page, and keep the choice in this browser; WHEN a link opens an issue of another project THE SYSTEM SHALL make that project current.
- **DB-N25-3** WHEN the account menu opens THE SYSTEM SHALL show *Switch workspace* in both setups; WHEN it opens THE SYSTEM SHALL list the workspaces `GET /api/workspaces` returns, each with its name, address and setup, the current one marked, and in Solo *Add a workspace…* and *Remove*, or in the Team setup *Open another workspace…*; WHEN a workspace is chosen THE SYSTEM SHALL open its address in this tab and send it nothing from this workspace ([teams](teams.md) TEAM-59).
- **DB-N25-4** WHEN Configuration says where a project's settings are THE SYSTEM SHALL name the project switcher, and the palette SHALL offer *Switch project…* and *Switch workspace…*.

### NEW-dashboard-26 — Many projects on one server: New project and the cross-project views ([DEC-57](../DECISIONS.md#dec-57--a-server-is-a-workspace-with-many-projects))
*Justification: D1 kept one project per server (DEC-53 c3), so *New project* on a server with its project said how to start another server, and My issues, the Inbox and search were tested with one project. DEC-57, the later ruling, puts many projects on one server: *New project* opens the start page or adopts a repository in any workspace, and the cross-project views must hold with several projects and per-project access. Owner workflow **C2b**.*

- **DB-N26-1** WHEN a person who may create a project presses *New project* in a workspace that already holds projects THE SYSTEM SHALL open the start page with the folder its approval creates ([design-stage](design-stage.md) DS-N8-1) and offer *Add an existing repository* ([design-stage](design-stage.md) DS-N8-2); WHEN anyone else presses it THE SYSTEM SHALL open the same page ending in *Send for approval* ([teams](teams.md) TEAM-57).
- **DB-N26-2** WHEN My issues, the Inbox or the palette's *Issues* lists items THE SYSTEM SHALL include every project the person can see and no other ([teams](teams.md) TEAM-58), show each row's project, and offer a project filter; WHEN a row of another project is opened THE SYSTEM SHALL make that project current (a test with two projects on one server, one of them hidden from a Member by an override).
- **DB-N26-3** WHEN a project is added while the active-project cap is reached THE SYSTEM SHALL show it on Projects as paused with the reason, and *Resume* SHALL be refused with the cap's sentence until a person pauses another ([runtime](runtime.md) RUN-82).

### NEW-dashboard-27 — Configuration › Models in three layers: the setup card, Customize per role, every value graded
*Justification: the owner asked for a Models page that a beginner can finish in one press and a professional can tune per role (W18, 2026-09-29), and C2a left Keep or Change, the checks in words and Compare setups' run from the card undone (DEV_LOG Entry 70). The page also showed and measured every model as the Coding model (FINDINGS CFG-13). The values, their grades and their effect on verification are [models](models.md) NEW-models-21's; the research is [MODEL_SETTINGS_2026-10](../../research/MODEL_SETTINGS_2026-10.md). Added by C3-3 under [DEC-56](../DECISIONS.md#dec-56--the-release-order-after-c1).*

- **DB-N27-1** WHEN Configuration › Models opens THE SYSTEM SHALL show the simple setup card first: one row per role in the order Coding, Planning, Review, Research, each with its job, its current model, the recommended model, *Keep* or *Change*, the checks in words, and *Customize*, and one *Apply suggestion* (*Use the recommended models*) whose confirmation states the total size of what it downloads and each model's licence before the person confirms.
- **DB-N27-2** WHEN a person opens *Customize* for a role THE SYSTEM SHALL show the tabs Basics, Sampling, Reasoning, Engine and Harness as one underlined tab bar, every value with its grade in words and its source, and *Reset* beside each value a person set; WHEN the context slider moves THE SYSTEM SHALL recompute the fit in words from the server's fit for that model, role and context, loading nothing.
- **DB-N27-3** WHEN a person saves a change to a value that is an element of the qualified combination THE SYSTEM SHALL show the role as *Needs verifying*, name the changed values, and offer *Verify now*, which runs the role's check (models rule 27a) and shows its result.
- **DB-N27-4** WHEN a value draws a hint THE SYSTEM SHALL show it beside the value and never disable *Save* for it; WHEN the server refuses a value (MD-N21-4) THE SYSTEM SHALL show the reason beside it and change nothing.
- **DB-N27-5** WHEN a person without the Admin level views Customize in the Team setup THE SYSTEM SHALL show every control read-only with the level note, and the server SHALL refuse any change with 403.
- **DB-N27-6** WHEN Models shows a model's details, its fit or *Measure speed* THE SYSTEM SHALL do so for the role the person chose, never the Coding model by default (FINDINGS CFG-13).
- **DB-N27-7** WHEN *Compare setups* is pressed on the card THE SYSTEM SHALL start the quick benchmark of the suggested combination with its estimate stated first, and show its state on the card.
- **DB-N27-8** WHEN the page is 400 px wide THE SYSTEM SHALL lay the setup card and Customize out with no sideways scroll, the tab bar scrolling within itself, and axe SHALL find no WCAG 2.2 A or AA violation at 1440 and 400 px.

### W9 — the §A performance budgets ([FINISH_LINE_PLAN](../../reference/FINISH_LINE_PLAN.md) §A, C4)
*The quality bar's Responsiveness rows had no measurement and no test (FINISH_LINE_PLAN B-15); W9 is C4's (the revised path's C4 row).*

- **DB-W9-1** WHEN the board, an issue page or Review is loaded at 1440 px on a board of 500 issues THE SYSTEM SHALL paint its largest content within 2.5 s (the median of five loads, LCP as Chromium reports it).
- **DB-W9-2** WHEN those pages load and are used THE SYSTEM SHALL keep their cumulative layout shift at or under 0.1.
- **DB-W9-3** WHEN a person clicks and presses keys on those pages THE SYSTEM SHALL answer at an INP-like p75 of 200 ms or less, each interaction's longest event counted, and one the browser does not report counted at 16 ms.

## 6. v1 acceptance

All criteria in §5, plus:

- **DB-1** WHEN Accept is pressed THE SYSTEM SHALL show a 3-second grace toast, and `z` within it SHALL cancel with no request sent.
- **DB-2** WHEN send back is submitted with an empty note THE SYSTEM SHALL block it client-side with *Add a note for the agent. It's what it reads next.*
- **DB-3** WHEN one shell state is active and another begins THE SYSTEM SHALL show only the higher-priority bar (ledger altered > offline > no Worker model > memory pause > review full > Worker paused for Seshat).
- **DB-4** WHEN the stream is silent for more than 10 s and `/api/meta` fails THE SYSTEM SHALL show *Offline since …*, freeze timestamps and disable every action.
- **DB-5** WHEN a stream frame changes one card THE SYSTEM SHALL patch that tile only, keeping scroll position, focus and any open drawer.
- **DB-6** WHEN a card is parked through the API THE SYSTEM SHALL show it in On hold within 1 s with the scroll position preserved.
- **DB-7** WHEN a Seshat reply mentions an id that is not a card THE SYSTEM SHALL render it as plain text.
- **DB-8** WHEN a proposal is stale THE SYSTEM SHALL disable its Apply and say why.
- **DB-9** WHEN 500 cards are loaded THE SYSTEM SHALL scroll the board with no main-thread task over 50 ms and under 50 MB of DOM memory.
- **DB-10** WHEN `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce` is set THE SYSTEM SHALL show no pulse and no translate.
- **DB-11** WHEN a tile is in a WIP-limited column at capacity THE SYSTEM SHALL colour its capacity bar amber, and red over the limit.
- **DB-12** WHEN Machine is open THE SYSTEM SHALL draw the memory, decode-speed and prefix-cache sparklines from samples taken every 5 s.
